 146: void crime_update_crime_time(struct crime_data* d)
 147: {
 148: 	struct timeval tv;
 149: 	
 150: 	gettimeofday(&tv, NULL);
 151: 
 152: 	uint64_t microseconds = tv.tv_sec * 1000000 + tv.tv_usec;
 153: 	if (d->last_microseconds == 0)
 154: 		d->last_microseconds = microseconds;
 155: 
 156: 	int64_t delta = microseconds - d->last_microseconds;
 157: 
 158: 	if (delta < 0) {
 159: 		fatal("[ crime_update_crime_time: host system time went backwards? ]\n");
 160: 		d->last_microseconds = microseconds;
 161: 		delta = 0;
 162: 	}
 163: 
 164: 	// The delta to add is 66 per microsecond.
 165: 	int64_t to_add = delta * 66;
 166: 
 167: 	if (to_add >= 1) {
 168: 		// NetBSD says CRIME_TIME_MASK = 0x0000ffffffffffffULL
 169: 		// but my O2 seems to use only the lower 32 bits as an
 170: 		// _unsigned_ value. (TODO: Double-check this again.)
 171: 		d->reg[CRIME_TIME / sizeof(uint64_t)] =
 172: 			(uint32_t)(d->reg[CRIME_TIME / sizeof(uint64_t)] + to_add);
 173: 
 174: 		d->last_microseconds = microseconds;
 175: 	}
 176: }

 184: DEVICE_TICK(crime)

 187:

 193: DEVICE_ACCESS(crime)
 194: {
 195: 	/*
 196: 	 *  The CRIME is memory mapped as 0x80 bytes, starting at
 197: 	 *  physical 0x14000000.
 198: 	 *
 199: 	 *  On my real O2:
 200: 	 *
 201: 	 *  0x14000080..0x140000ff is the same as 0x14000000..0x1400007f.
 202: 	 *  0x14000100..0x1400017f is the same as 0x14000000..0x1400007f.
 203: 	 *  0x14000180..0x140001ff is the same as 0x14000000..0x1400007f.
 204: 	 *
 205: 	 *  (I assume that real code will never access the above "mirrors",
 206: 	 *  but it would be trivial to add redirection so that such code
 207: 	 *  would work too.)
 208: 	 *
 209: 	 *  Memory control then starts at 0x14000200.
 210: 	 */
 211: 	struct crime_data *d = (struct crime_data *) extra;
 212: 	uint64_t idata = 0, odata = 0;
 213: 	uint64_t preserved_CRIME_HARDINT = d->reg[CRIME_HARDINT / sizeof(uint64_t)];
 214: 	size_t i;
 215: 
 216: 	if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
 217: 		idata = memory_readmax64(cpu, data, len);
 218: 
 219: 	/*
 220: 	 *  Set crime version/revision:
 221: 	 *
 222: 	 *  This might not be the most elegant or correct solution, but it
 223: 	 *  seems that the IP32 PROM likes 0x11 for machines without graphics,
 224: 	 *  and 0xa1 for machines with graphics.
 225: 	 *
 226: 	 *  NetBSD 2.0 complains about "unknown" crime for 0x11, but I guess
 227: 	 *  that's something one has to live with.  (TODO?)
 228: 	 */
 229: 	d->reg[CRIME_REV / sizeof(uint64_t)] = d->use_fb? 0xa1 : 0x11;
 230: 
 231: 	d->reg[CRIME_CONTROL / sizeof(uint64_t)] = CRIME_CONTROL_ENDIANESS;
 232: 
 233: 	d->reg[CRIME_INTSTAT / sizeof(uint64_t)] =
 234: 		(d->reg[CRIME_SOFTINT / sizeof(uint64_t)] |
 235: 		d->reg[CRIME_HARDINT / sizeof(uint64_t)]);
 236: 
 237: 	/*
 238: 	 *  Amount of memory.  Bit 8 of bank control set ==> 128MB instead
 239: 	 *  of 32MB per bank (?)
 240: 	 *
 241: 	 *  According to OpenBSD, "Empty banks are reported as duplicates of
 242: 	 *  bank #0."
 243: 	 */
 244: 	if (cpu->machine->physical_ram_in_mb > 1024) {
 245: 		fatal("[ sgi_crime: SGI O2 can not have more than 1024 MB RAM ]\n");
 246: 		exit(1);
 247: 	}
 248: 
 249: 	int mb_per_bank;
 250: 	if (cpu->machine->physical_ram_in_mb <= 256)
 251: 		mb_per_bank = 32;
 252: 	else
 253: 		mb_per_bank = 128;
 254: 
 255: 	if (cpu->machine->physical_ram_in_mb % mb_per_bank) {
 256: 		fatal("[ sgi_crime: for up to 256 MB RAM, RAM size needs to be divisible "
 257: 			"by 32 MB. for larger RAM sizes (up to 1024 MB), it needs to be "
 258: 			"divisible by 128 MB. ]\n");
 259: 		exit(1);
 260: 	}
 261: 
 262: 	int flag_for_128MB = mb_per_bank == 128 ? 0x100 : 0x000;
 263: 	size_t total_mb = 0;
 264: 	for (int bank = 0; bank < 8; ++bank) {
 265: 		int b = mb_per_bank == 128 ? (bank << 2) : bank;
 266: 		if (total_mb >= cpu->machine->physical_ram_in_mb)
 267: 			d->reg[CRIME_MEM_BANK_CTRL0 / sizeof(uint64_t) + bank] =
 268: 				d->reg[CRIME_MEM_BANK_CTRL0 / sizeof(uint64_t) + 0];
 269: 		else
 270: 			d->reg[CRIME_MEM_BANK_CTRL0 / sizeof(uint64_t) + bank] = flag_for_128MB | b;
 271: 
 272: 		total_mb += mb_per_bank;
 273: 	}
 274: 	
 275: 	if (len == 8) {
 276: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
 277: 			d->reg[relative_addr / 8] = idata;
 278: 		else
 279: 			odata = d->reg[relative_addr / 8];
 280: 	} else if (len == 4) {
 281: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 282: 			if (relative_addr & 4) {
 283: 				d->reg[relative_addr / 8] &= ~0xffffffffULL;
 284: 				d->reg[relative_addr / 8] |= (uint32_t)idata;
 285: 			} else {
 286: 				d->reg[relative_addr / 8] &= 0xffffffffULL;
 287: 				d->reg[relative_addr / 8] |= (uint64_t)(idata << 32ULL);
 288: 			}
 289: 		} else {
 290: 			odata = d->reg[relative_addr / 8];
 291: 			if (relative_addr & 4)
 292: 				odata = (int32_t)odata;
 293: 			else
 294: 				odata = (int32_t)(odata >> 32);
 295: 		}
 296: 	} else {
 297: 		/*
 298: 		 *  #529: THIS USED TO END THE PROCESS.  One guest lb, lbu, lh,
 299: 		 *  lhu, sb or sh anywhere in CRIME did it (witnessed at rung 3
 300: 		 *  on an unmodified -E sgi -e ip32, 6/6).  The width is now
 301: 		 *  DECLINED and announced once, the #498 sgi_de shape: a read
 302: 		 *  is answered 0 through the common tail, a write is dropped.
 303: 		 *
 304: 		 *  The goto skips the register switch below, and that is the
 305: 		 *  point of it: falling through would let a halfword 0x0800
 306: 		 *  written at CRIME_CONTROL stop every CPU, a narrow access to
 307: 		 *  CRIME_REV from the PROM probe loop redirect the PC, one at
 308: 		 *  INTSTAT or INTMASK reassert the interrupt line, and one at
 309: 		 *  CRIME_TIME advance the clock -- all measured.  It is not
 310: 		 *  `return 0' either: that raises a guest bus error
 311: 		 *  (EXCEPTION_DBE, cpus/memory_rw.c), and what real CRIME does
 312: 		 *  on a narrow cycle is not documented here.
 313: 		 */
 314: 		if (!(d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN)) {
 315: 			d->warned |= CRIME_W_LEN;
 316: 			fatal("[ crime: unimplemented len=%i at 0x%x -- declined."
 317: 			    "  (once) ]\n", (int) len, (int) relative_addr);
 318: 		}
 319: 		goto crime_declined;
 320: 	}
 321: 
 322: 	switch (relative_addr) {
 323: 
 324: 	case CRIME_REV:		/*  0x000  */
 325: 		/*
 326: 		 *  A contender for winning a prize for the worst hack
 327: 		 *  in history:  the IP32 PROM probes the CPU caches during
 328: 		 *  bootup, but they are not really emulated, so it fails.
 329: 		 *  During the probe, the CRIME_REV is read a lot. By
 330: 		 *  "returning" from the probe function, i.e. jumping to ra,
 331: 		 *  when this register is read the second time, the probe
 332: 		 *  can be skipped, and the PROM thus runs further.
 333: 		 *
 334: 		 *  The address where this happens is:
 335: 		 *	0xbfc0517c	PROM v2.3
 336: 		 *	0xbfc051ac	PROM v4.13
 337: 		 *
 338: 		 *  4.18 works too.
 339: 		 *
 340: 		 *  By extrapolating a bit (allowing for variations for other
 341: 		 *  versions of the PROM), let's return if the read of the
 342: 		 *  CRIME_REV register occurs anywhere near 0xbfc051XX.
 343: 		 */
 344: 		if (((uint32_t)cpu->pc & 0xffffff00) == (uint32_t)0xbfc05100) {
 345: 			cpu->pc = cpu->cd.mips.gpr[MIPS_GPR_RA];
 346: 			if (cpu->is_32bit)
 347: 				mips32_pc_to_pointers(cpu);
 348: 			else
 349: 				mips_pc_to_pointers(cpu);
 350: 		}
 351: 		break;
 352: 
 353: 	case CRIME_CONTROL:	/*  0x008  */
 354: 		/*  TODO: 64-bit write to CRIME_CONTROL, but some things
 355: 		    (such as NetBSD 1.6.2) write to 0x00c!  */
 356: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 357: 			/*
 358: 			 *  0x200 = watchdog timer (according to NetBSD)
 359: 			 *  0x800 = "reboot" used by the IP32 PROM
 360: 			 */
 361: 			if (idata & CRIME_CONTROL_DOG_ENABLE) {
 362: 				idata &= ~CRIME_CONTROL_DOG_ENABLE;
 363: 			}
 364: 			if (idata & CRIME_CONTROL_HARD_RESET) {
 365: 				int j;
 366: 
 367: 				/*  This is used by the IP32 PROM's
 368: 				    "reboot" command:  */
 369: 				for (j=0; j<cpu->machine->ncpus; j++)
 370: 					cpu->machine->cpus[j]->running = 0;
 371: 
 372: 				idata &= ~CRIME_CONTROL_HARD_RESET;
 373: 			}
 374: 
 375: 			idata &= ~CRIME_CONTROL_ENDIANESS;
 376: 
 377: 			if (idata != 0)
 378: 				fatal("[ CRIME_CONTROL: unimplemented "
 379: 				    "control 0x%016llx ]\n", (long long)idata);
 380: 		}
 381: 		break;
 382: 
 383: 	case CRIME_INTSTAT:	/*  0x010, Current interrupt status  */
 384: 	case CRIME_INTMASK:	/*  0x018, Current interrupt mask  */
 385: 		crime_interrupt_reassert(d);
 386: 		break;
 387: 
 388: 	case CRIME_DOG:		/*  0x030  */
 389: 		// No warning.
 390: 		break;
 391: 
 392: 	case CRIME_TIME:	/*  0x038  */
 393: 		crime_update_crime_time(d);
 394: 		break;
 395: 
 396: 	default:
 397: 		if (writeflag==MEM_READ) {
 398: 			debug("[ crime: read from 0x%x, len=%i:",
 399: 			    (int)relative_addr, len);
 400: 			for (i=0; i<len; i++)
 401: 				debug(" %02x", data[i]);
 402: 			debug(" ]\n");
 403: 		} else {
 404: 			debug("[ crime: write to 0x%x:", (int)relative_addr);
 405: 			for (i=0; i<len; i++)
 406: 				debug(" %02x", data[i]);
 407: 			debug(" (len=%i) ]\n", len);
 408: 		}
 409: 	}
 410: 
 411:  crime_declined:
 412: 	/*  #529: a declined access jumps here, skipping the register switch,
 413: 	    and is still answered.  */
 414: 	d->reg[CRIME_HARDINT / sizeof(uint64_t)] = preserved_CRIME_HARDINT;
 415: 	d->reg[CRIME_INTSTAT / sizeof(uint64_t)] =
 416: 		(d->reg[CRIME_SOFTINT / sizeof(uint64_t)] |
 417: 		d->reg[CRIME_HARDINT / sizeof(uint64_t)]);
 418: 
 419: 	if (writeflag == MEM_READ)
 420: 		memory_writemax64(cpu, data, len, odata);
 421: 
 422: 	return 1;
 423: }

 426: /*
 427:  *  dev_crime_init():
 428:  */
 429: void dev_crime_init(struct machine *machine, struct memory *mem,
 430: 	uint64_t baseaddr, char *irq_path, int use_fb)
 431: {
 432: 	struct crime_data *d;

 445: 		struct interrupt templ;

 331: RA = 0x80004000
 332: PROBE_PC = 0xbfc05180           # inside the CRIME_REV hack's window, (pc & 0xffffff00) == 0xbfc05100

 334: R = {"zero": 0, "at": 1, "v0": 2, "v1": 3, "a0": 4, "a1": 5, "a2": 6, "a3": 7, "t0": 8, "t1": 9,
 335:      "s0": 16, "s1": 17, "s2": 18, "s3": 19, "ra": 31}
 336: OPC = {"lb": 0x20, "lh": 0x21, "lw": 0x23, "lbu": 0x24, "lhu": 0x25, "ld": 0x37,

 341: 
 342: def enc(op, rs, rt, imm=0):
 343:     return (OPC[op] << 26) | (R[rs] << 21) | (R[rt] << 16) | (imm & 0xffff)
 344: 
 345: 
 346: #  hand-assembled encodings checked against the committed witness's constants (a wrong register field reads 0, which
 347: #  a decline's own answer would accept by accident -- so the accept rows carry nonzero wants)
 348: assert enc("lb", "at", "v0") == 0x80220000 and enc("lhu", "at", "v0") == 0x94220000
 349: assert enc("sb", "a3", "t0") == 0xa0e80000 and enc("sh", "a3", "t0") == 0xa4e80000
 350: assert enc("sw", "a3", "t0") == 0xace80000 and enc("lw", "at", "v0") == 0x8c220000

 354: 
 355: def row(name, got, want, note=""):
 356:     ok = (got == want)
 357:     rows.append((name, ok))
 358:     print("%-16s %-20s %-20s %-40s %s" % (name, str(got), str(want), note, "ok" if ok else "FAIL"))
 359:     sys.stdout.flush()

 361: 
 362: def session(cmds, settle=1.5, spin=1.2):
 363:     """one emulator, read to the pty's EOF after `quit'.  Returns (text, clean): clean = EOF reached AND exit 0."""
 364:     pid, fd = pty.fork()
 365:     if pid == 0:
 366:         os.execv(BIN, [BIN, "-V", "-x", "-E", "sgi", "-e", "ip32", "-M", "64", "0x80001000:" + DUMMY])
 367:         os._exit(127)
 368:     buf = ""
 369: 
 370:     def rd(t):
 371:         nonlocal buf
 372:         end = time.time() + t
 373:         while time.time() < end:
 374:             r, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], 0.1)
 375:             if not r:
 376:                 continue
 377:             try:
 378:                 d = os.read(fd, 65536)
 379:             except OSError:
 380:                 return False
 381:             if not d:
 382:                 return False
 383:             buf += d.decode("latin-1", "replace")
 384:         return True
 385: 
 386:     rd(settle)
 387:     for i, c in enumerate(cmds):
 388:         try:
 389:             if c == "<RUN>":        # free-run: `continue', let the guest run, then break back in with CTRL-C
 390:                 os.write(fd, b"continue\n")
 391:                 ok = rd(2.0)
 392:                 os.write(fd, b"\x03")
 393:                 ok = ok and rd(1.5)
 394:             else:
 395:                 os.write(fd, (c + "\n").encode("latin-1"))
 396:                 ok = rd(spin if i == len(cmds) - 1 else 0.25)
 397:         except OSError:
 398:             break
 399:         if not ok:
 400:             break
 401:     eof = True                      # a process already gone before `quit' has closed the pty; its status says how
 402:     try:
 403:         os.write(fd, b"quit\n")
 404:         eof = not rd(10.0)          # to EOF, so anything printed on the way out is in the transcript
 405:     except OSError:
 406:         pass
 407:     if not eof:
 408:         try:
 409:             os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)    # a session that never reached EOF is ended, never waited on forever
 410:         except OSError:
 411:             pass
 412:     try:
 413:         os.close(fd)
 414:     except OSError:
 415:         pass
 416:     how = "nostatus"
 417:     try:
 418:         _, stat = os.waitpid(pid, 0)
 419:         how = ("sig%d" % os.WTERMSIG(stat)) if os.WIFSIGNALED(stat) else ("exit%d" % os.WEXITSTATUS(stat))
 420:     except OSError:
 421:         pass
 422:     return buf.replace("\r", ""), (eof and how == "exit0")

 424: 
 425: def pr(t, reg):
 426:     m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
 427:     return ("0x%x" % int(m.group(1), 16)) if m else "absent"
 428: 
 429: 
 430: def exc(t):
 431:     c = pr(t, "cause")
 432:     return ("%d" % ((int(c, 16) >> 2) & 0x1f)) if c != "absent" else "absent"

 434: 
 435: def count(t, s, clean):
 436:     """a text count is evidence only from a whole transcript: an unclean session reads `unclean', never a number"""
 437:     return t.count(s) if clean else "unclean"

 439: 
 440: class Addr(object):
 441:     def __init__(self, base):
 442:         self.a = base
 443: 
 444:     def take(self, n):
 445:         got = self.a
 446:         self.a += 4 * (n + 2) + 0x20
 447:         return got
 448: 
 449: 
 450: def run_ops(ops, at):
 451:     put = ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, op) for i, op in enumerate(ops)]
 452:     put += ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * len(ops), OP_B_SELF),
 453:             "put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * len(ops) + 4, OP_NOP)]
 454:     return put + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(ops)]
 455: 
 456: 
 457: def ld(A, op, addr, rt, at=None):
 458:     """one load `op rt, 0(at)', rt seeded with SENT; returns (cmds, code address)"""
 459:     here = A.take(1) if at is None else at
 460:     return ["at=0x%x" % addr, "%s=0x%x" % (rt, SENT)] + run_ops([enc(op, "at", rt)], here), here
 461: 
 462: 
 463: def st(A, op, addr, val, at=None):
 464:     here = A.take(1) if at is None else at
 465:     return ["a3=0x%x" % addr, "t0=0x%x" % val] + run_ops([enc(op, "a3", "t0")], here), here

 468: AFTER = ["print pc", "print cause"]
 469: 
 470: 
 471: def roundtrip(A):
 472:     """a RAM round trip printed in v1: absent if the host died, the sentinel if the CPU was stopped"""
 473:     c1, _ = st(A, "sw", SCRATCH_RAM, LIVE_VAL)
 474:     c2, _ = ld(A, "lw", SCRATCH_RAM, "v1")
 475:     return c1 + c2 + ["print v1"]

 478: def said(t, ln, off, clean):
 479:     """the whole announce, `(once)' marker included -- a reworded or unmarked announce reads 0"""
 480:     return count(t, "unimplemented len=%d at 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]" % (ln, off), clean)
 481: 
 482: 
 483: def dflt(t, clean, off, write):
 484:     """the register switch's default-arm debug() line FOR THIS REFUSED ACCESS -- `[ crime: read from 0xOFF,' or
 485:     `[ crime: write to 0xOFF:' -- printed while stepping, so a refused access that falls into the switch at an
 486:     unlabelled offset says so here.  Keyed on the access's own offset and direction, because an ACCEPTED access at a
 487:     default-arm offset in the same session prints the same kind of line (hi keeps' ld +0x278 does)."""
 488:     if not clean:
 489:         return "unclean"
 490:     return t.count(("[ crime: write to 0x%x:" if write else "[ crime: read from 0x%x,") % off)

 102: 
 103: ## 2.  THE FIND, PINNED
 104: 
 105: ### (a) HOW GENERAL THE CLASS IS -- derived from the handler, cell by cell
 106: 
 107: A silent decline at an accepted cell removes exactly three things: the `d->reg[]` update (writes), the `odata` it would
 108: have answered (reads), and the switch arm's side effect.  Working those against the function
 109: (`dev_sgi_ip32.c:229-231` prelude, `:275-320` width arms, `:322-409` switch, `:411-420` tail) gives, over the 46
 110: `ACCEPTS` entries:
 111: 
 112: * **EQUIVALENT -- nothing observable at all (10 entries):** REV 8W / 4W-hi / 4R-hi out of the window (the prelude
 113:   rewrites `reg[0]`, its high half is 0); CONTROL 4R-hi (high half of 0x100 is 0); CONTROL 4W and 8W at `0`, `0x0200`
 114:   and `0x0100` (DOG is a local, ENDIANESS is cleared unconditionally, the leftover is 0 so nothing prints).
 115: * **HOST-DIAGNOSTIC ONLY -- the guest cannot tell (5 entries):** CONTROL 4W and 8W at `0x0201` and `0x0001` (the
 116:   leftover-nonzero `fatal()` line), and the default-arm 4W at `+0x04` (its `debug()` write line; the store itself lands
 117:   in REV's low lane, which the prelude rewrites).
 118: * **GUEST-VISIBLE (31 entries):** everything else.  The channels are the PROM-window redirect (`pc = ra`, the four
 119:   window kinds), a nonzero register answer (REV 0x11, CONTROL 0x100, INTSTAT, INTMASK, DOG, TIME, the far and middle
 120:   blocks), a durable store read back later (INTMASK, DOG, TIME, every default-arm slot), `crime_interrupt_reassert()`
 121:   raising or lowering `cause.IP2` (INTSTAT and INTMASK, all four kinds each), and the skipped
 122:   `crime_update_crime_time()` (TIME, all four kinds).
 123: 
 124: **Every one of the 31 is reachable in the K form**, because the mutant's condition is just `(d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN)`
 125: and any session that has refused once satisfies it.  So the class is not three cells; it is thirty-one.
 126: 
 127: ### (b) WHY NO SHIPPED ROW SEES IT -- and exactly which eight it does see
 128: 
 129: From S1's session map: of the 31 guest-visible cells, **eight are exercised post-latch with an oracle** and
 130: **twenty-three are not**.  `rearm once` (#26) runs all 46 post-latch and is the reason the mutant is *reachable* in the
 131: shipped probe -- and it counts notices only, so it is blind by construction.  `latch x5` (#40) runs its 46 BEFORE its
 132: first refusal, so the K mutant is inert there and `latch said` stays green.
 133: 
 134: That is why K1/K2/K3 score 157/157 while a PRE/POST discriminator shows a guest difference at each
 135: (`logs/disc_K*.out`): INTMASK 4W lost (`0x0` vs `0x1000000000000000`), the REV window redirect lost (pc `0xbfc05184`
 136: vs ra), CONTROL 8R answering `0x0` vs `0x100` -- with the notice count 1 in every case.
 137: 
 138: > **The one sentence that generalises it:** the shipped detector measures the **latch-CLEAR** half of
 139: > {latch} x {len} x {direction} x {label} at every cell and the **latch-SET** half at eight cells.  Table 10 closed two
 140: > more cells of that product (`W_8r`, `W_8w`) at the WIDTH level, with no label conditioning.  **Adding the label
 141: > conditioning is what escapes**, and the dispatch label is a floor axis here by the 2026-09-09 `sgimtedepth4dead`
 142: > ruling, because `relative_addr` flows into `d->reg[relative_addr / 8]`, an lvalue the guarded body both reads and
 143: > writes.
 144: 
 145: **Why this is FLOOR and not clause-(e) debt** -- the ruling's RA3 says the `d->warned`-keyed shrink is *"clause (e) by
 146: the DEPENDS-ON bound (`d->warned` is not a field the accept bodies read)"*.  I hold that reading is wrong, and I state
 147: the counter-argument because it is the only thing standing between GAP and DEBT:
 148: 
 149: * the clause-(d) operand-swap form draws swaps from *"fields the guarded BODY reads"*, and the guarded body of the
 150:   width dispatch is the whole `if (len == 8) / else if (len == 4) / else` chain.  **Its `else` arm reads `d->warned`**
 151:   (`:314`) -- a read this very fix ADDED.  The narrow reading in RA3 scopes "body" to the two ACCEPT arms only.
 152: * the 2026-09-04 JOINT-CELLS wording enumerates joint cells *"exactly when the guarded body's own code distinguishes
 153:   them"*; `if (!(d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN))` is literally that test, so `{clear, set}` is a body-tested partition.
 154: * the round's own practice already agrees: RA3 **ordered the mutant BUILT** rather than disclosed, and table 10 built
 155:   and scored it.  A class that owes a measured mutant is not being treated as prose.
 156: 
 157: Honest limit: RA3's narrow reading is defensible on its own terms, and if the adjudicator upholds it the disposition is
 158: clause-(e) debt with three MEASURED escapes named -- not silence.  **Either way the record must stop saying the silent
 159: form is "CAUGHT" at these cells, because it is measurably not.**
 160: 
 161: ### (c) THE CHEAPEST CLOSURE -- prototyped in a COPY and MEASURED
 162: 
 163: `scripts2/post7.py` is a standalone prototype; the shipped probe was not edited (md5 identical before and after).  It
 164: repeats the existing value checks ONCE with the latch set -- the whole structural change is one refused `lb +0x07` at
 165: the head of the session.  Frozen before the comparison (`logs2/freeze.out`), every log ends `POST7_DONE`.
 166: 
 167: | binary | predicted (`PREDICT2.md`, written first) | **measured** | rows red |
 168: |---|---|---|---|
 169: | base | 23/23 | **29/29** | -- |
 170: | K1_w_imask4w | 22/23 {p acc imask sw} | **28/29** | `p acc imask sw` 0x0 |

 175: 
 176: *(The denominator is 29, not the 23 I predicted: I under-counted my own control rows.  Recorded as a miss.)*
 177: 
 178: **The three K escapes close, each on its own cell, with no new channel invented and no control disturbed.**  The four
 179: negative controls stay green, so the rows measure the mutant and not the POST prefix.
 180: 
 181: #### The TIME 4W/4R cell does NOT close with this template, and finding that out took three shapes
 182: 
 183: My `p time 4w/4r upd` rows were **VACUOUS**, which is the CHANNEL-line failure this project's pre-table block exists
 184: to catch, arriving inside my own repair prototype.  Measured, then repaired, then repaired again:
 185: 
 186: | shape | base | E2 | control | disposition |
 187: |---|---|---|---|---|
 188: | `post7`: POST prefix, then `ld; 4-byte; ld`, magnitude threshold | 0x10d49672 | 0x86c913c | -- | **VACUOUS** -- a factor of 2, both far above the threshold |
 189: | `post7b` FREE: one run of `[lb, ld, 4-byte, ld]` | 0x84, 0x42 | **0x0, 0x0** | K1 0xc6, **0x0** | **FLAKY** -- a FALSE RED on a control |
 190: | `post7b` STEP: one `step 4` of the same | 0xfba, 0x1188 | 0x8c4, 0xa92 | K1 0xce4, 0xca2 | **NON-DISCRIMINATING** |
 191: | **`post7c` GAP: `step 2 [lb, ld]` then `step 1 [4-byte]` then `step 1 [ld]`** | **0x13414f8, 0x1348fb0** (2 runs) | **0x5ee, 0x5ac** | K1 0x13562d6/0x135de96, K3 0x1355f7c/0x135c6de | **WORKS** -- ~13,000x separation, no false red |
 192: 
 193: **Cause, established rather than guessed:** the POST prefix's own `step` runs the CPU, so `dev_crime_tick` fires and
 194: sets `d->last_microseconds`.  After that every accepted TIME access only CATCHES UP, so skipping one HALVES the total
 195: instead of zeroing it.  The gapped shape fuses the refusal and the first TIME read into the session's first `step`, so
 196: TIME is still exactly 0 after it (`first ld 0x0` on all four legs) and the 4-byte access is the only thing that can
 197: move it before the final read.
 198: 
 199: *Log-hygiene note: `laneR.sh` ran `base` twice into the same filename, so `logs2/post7c_base.out` holds run 2; both
 200: scores are preserved in `logs2/laneR.out` and the run-1 raw values are not.  My defect, recorded.*
 201: 
 202: ### The cells the closure leaves, and the cheapest way to take them
 203: 
 204: Of the 46 `ACCEPTS` entries, the prototype (4 value sessions + 2 TIME sessions, 30 rows) plus the 8 already covered
 205: would pin **32** post-latch.  The 14 left are the 10 EQUIVALENTs (no row owed) and four others:

 succeeded in 229ms:

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""mutants529k.py NAME SRC DST -- #529 KILL TABLE 11 (repair 13).

Imports table 10's mutator (every earlier mutant by name) and adds the closing-pass-7 findings plus THE CLASS CLOSURE.

*** THE CLOSURE IS GENERATED FROM THE HANDLER'S OWN CASE LABELS, NOT TRANSCRIBED. ***  The 2026-08-31 class-closure
amendment says: when two consecutive passes find breaches of ONE class, the next repair enumerates the full space FROM
THE FUNCTION'S OWN INPUTS and closes every cell in one diff, and the closing pass then VERIFIES THE ENUMERATION rather
than hunting cells.  Three passes have now found this class (pass 6 the ANNOUNCED accept-guard growth; table 10 the
WIDTH-level latch shrink `W_8r'/`W_8w'; pass 7 the PER-CELL latch shrink K1/K2/K3), so the enumeration is owed.

THE SPACE, read off `DEVICE_ACCESS(crime)' in dev_sgi_ip32.c:

    {latch}     d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN in {clear, set}
                  -- the width dispatch's own `else' arm READS this field (`if (!(d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN))'), and that
                     read is part of the fix's OWN diff.  Whether that makes it a FLOOR axis (the sgimtedepth4dead join
                     ruling) or clause-(e) debt (the ruling's RA3) is the one question still open for the adjudicator.
    {len}       4, 8            -- the two accepted widths; every other width is the declined arm itself
    {dir}       read, write     -- `writeflag'
    {label}     the SEVEN the switch actually distinguishes: CRIME_REV, CRIME_CONTROL, CRIME_INTSTAT, CRIME_INTMASK,
                CRIME_DOG, CRIME_TIME, and `default' (which is where 0x20 SOFTINT, 0x40 and +0x278 land)

    2 x 2 x 2 x 7 = 56 cells.  THE LATCH-CLEAR HALF IS ALREADY MEASURED: table 10's twelve S_* silent bypasses are
    exactly that half at the cells the ruling enumerated, all red.  **The NEW work is the 28 latch-SET cells**, which
    is what K1/K2/K3 are three instances of.

*** DO NOT READ 28 AS THE ROW DEMAND.  MUTANTS AND CELLS ARE DIFFERENT UNITS. ***  One K-form mutant covers a
(len, dir, label) triple that may span several ACCEPTS entries, and the close-7 seat's static session map
(opus_close7_20260915/scripts2/session_map2.py) derives the row side independently: of the 46 ACCEPTS entries 31 are
guest-visible, and 8 of those are ALREADY pinned post-latch, leaving 23.  A mutant at a cell no row reaches is a
measurement that teaches nothing; a row at a cell no mutant reaches is unfalsifiable.  Both lists go in PRETABLE 11
with the CHANNEL line naming, per row, the mutant it kills and the observable that differs -- **and this round's
evidence is that the CHANNEL line must be RUN, not written: the seat's first two TIME shapes were named correctly and
were VACUOUS, and one gave a FALSE RED on a control.**

Every find-text occurs EXACTLY ONCE; the driver treats MUTANT-TEXT-NOT-FOUND as a FAIL, so each find is also a
presence pin on the feature it edits.
"""
import io, os, sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
for t in ("killtable11_20260916", "killtable10_20260915", "killtable9_20260914"):
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(HERE, "..", t))
import mutants529j as J                                    # noqa: E402

M = dict(J.M)

LEN8 = "\tif (len == 8) {\n"
LEN4 = "\t} else if (len == 4) {\n"
W, R_ = "writeflag == MEM_WRITE", "writeflag == MEM_READ"

#  ---- the three the close-7 seat MEASURED, verbatim (each 157/157 against repair 12) ----------------------------
M.update({
    "K1": [(LEN4, "\t} else if (len == 4 && !(%s &&\n\t    relative_addr == CRIME_INTMASK && (d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN))) {\n" % W)],
    "K2": [(LEN4, "\t} else if (len == 4 && !(relative_addr == CRIME_REV && (d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN))) {\n")],
    "K3": [(LEN8, "\tif (len == 8 && !(%s &&\n\t    relative_addr == CRIME_CONTROL && (d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN))) {\n" % R_)],
    #  codex's TIME mutant, verbatim: the cell item 6 and the ruling's RA1 both called EQUIVALENT and which is
    #  measured guest-visible on four channels.  Killed only by the GAPPED row shape.
    "E2": J.bypass("len == 4 && relative_addr == CRIME_TIME"),
})

#  ---- THE CLOSURE, GENERATED.  28 latch-set cells; the generator IS the enumeration. ---------------------------
LABELS = (("rev", "relative_addr == CRIME_REV"),
          ("ctl", "relative_addr == CRIME_CONTROL"),
          ("ist", "relative_addr == CRIME_INTSTAT"),
          ("imk", "relative_addr == CRIME_INTMASK"),
          ("dog", "relative_addr == CRIME_DOG"),
          ("tim", "relative_addr == CRIME_TIME"),
          #  the `default' arm is the COMPLEMENT of the six, and it is written as one so the mutant cannot be
          #  satisfied by a label the switch already names -- the enumeration's own negative space.
          ("dfl", "relative_addr != CRIME_REV && relative_addr != CRIME_CONTROL &&\n\t    "
                  "relative_addr != CRIME_INTSTAT && relative_addr != CRIME_INTMASK &&\n\t    "
                  "relative_addr != CRIME_DOG && relative_addr != CRIME_TIME"))


def klatch(anchor, head, dir_expr, label_expr):
    """the K-form: keep the width guard, null its repair at ONE cell ONCE THE LATCH HAS FIRED (silent by construction,
    because the announce is what the latch suppresses)"""
    return [(anchor, "%s && !(%s && (%s) &&\n\t    (d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN))) {\n" % (head, dir_expr, label_expr))]


for lab, expr in LABELS:
    for ln, anchor, head in ((8, LEN8, "\tif (len == 8"), (4, LEN4, "\t} else if (len == 4")):

#  MEASURED.  The generated C_rev4w / C_rev4r are its two halves and are kept: a cell whose two halves are covered
#  separately is not thereby covered jointly, and this table is the one that says so.

if __name__ == "__main__":

        print(" ".join(M)); sys.exit(0)

    name, src, dst = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
    s = io.open(src, newline="").read()

        if n != 1:

        s = s.replace(find, repl, 1)
    io.open(dst, "w", newline="").write(s)
    print("APPLIED %s (%d edit(s))" % (name, len(M[name])))

import ast, re, sys, io, contextlib, pathlib, collections, hashlib
root = pathlib.Path('.')
path = root/'regress/crime_width_probe.py'
source = path.read_text()
tree = ast.parse(source)
sessions = []
namespace = {'BIN':'STATIC-NO-EMULATOR', 'LABEL':'STATIC', 're':re, 'sys':sys}
def audit_session(cmds, settle=1.5, spin=1.2):
    sessions.append({'commands':list(cmds), 'rows':[]})
    return '', True
def audit_row(name, got, want, note=''):
    sessions[-1]['rows'].append(name)
    namespace['rows'].append((name,False))
namespace.update(audit_session=audit_session, audit_row=audit_row)
body = []
for node in tree.body:
    if node.lineno < 326:
        continue
    if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef) and node.name in ('session','row'):
        args = 'cmds, settle, spin' if node.name == 'session' else 'name, got, want, note'
        node.body = ast.parse('return audit_' + node.name + '(' + args + ')').body
    body.append(node)
tree.body = body
ast.fix_missing_locations(tree)
with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    exec(compile(tree, str(path), 'exec'), namespace)
ops = {v:k for k,v in namespace['OPC'].items()}
regids = namespace['R']
events = []
for sid, sess in enumerate(sessions):
    regs, code, pc, latch = {0:0}, {}, None, False
    for cmd in sess['commands']:
        m = re.fullmatch(r'([a-z0-9]+)=0x([0-9a-f]+)',cmd)
        if m:
            if m[1] == 'pc': pc = int(m[2],16)
            elif m[1] in regids: regs[regids[m[1]]] = int(m[2],16)
            continue
        m = re.fullmatch(r'put w 0x([0-9a-f]+), 0x([0-9a-f]{8})',cmd)
        if m:
            code[int(m[1],16)] = int(m[2],16)
            continue
        if cmd.startswith('step ') or cmd == '<RUN>':
            steps = int(cmd.split()[1]) if cmd.startswith('step ') else 32
            for _ in range(steps):
                ins = code.get(pc,0)
                op = ops.get(ins>>26)
                if op:
                    rs, rt, imm = (ins>>21)&31, (ins>>16)&31, ins&65535
                    if imm&32768: imm -= 65536
                    addr = regs.get(rs,0)+imm
                    size = {'b':1,'h':2,'w':4,'d':8}[op[1]]
                    if 0xb4000000 <= addr < 0xb4000280:
                        ev = {'session':sid, 'op':op, 'size':size, 'offset':addr-0xb4000000, 'pc':pc,
                              'value':regs.get(rt,0) if op.startswith('s') else None, 'latch':latch}
                        events.append(ev)
                        if size in (1,2): latch = True
                if ins == namespace['OP_B_SELF']: break
                if pc is not None: pc += 4
control = [e for e in events if e['offset']==8 and e['op'] in ('sw','sd')]
reset = [e for e in control if e['value']&0x800]
print('STATIC ONLY: sessions=%d rows=%d ACCEPTS=%d' % (len(sessions),len(namespace['rows']),len(namespace['ACCEPTS'])))
print('Accepted CONTROL writes with HARD_RESET set:', reset)
print('Post-latch accepted CONTROL write values:', sorted(set((e['op'],hex(e['value'])) for e in control if e['latch'])))
print('Candidate post-latch 4W CONTROL HARD_RESET trigger count:',sum(e['latch'] and e['op']=='sw' for e in reset))
print('latch said session:',next((i,s['rows']) for i,s in enumerate(sessions) if 'latch said' in s['rows']))
latid = next(i for i,s in enumerate(sessions) if 'latch said' in s['rows'])
lat = [e for e in events if e['session']==latid]
print('latch said first refusal index:',next(i for i,e in enumerate(lat) if e['size'] in (1,2)))
print('latch said first refusal:',next(e for e in lat if e['size'] in (1,2)))
old = ast.parse((root/'regress/artifacts/crimewidthkill/killtable10_20260915/crime_width_probe.table10.py').read_text())
new = ast.parse(source)
def prefix(tr, stop):
    out=[]
    for node in tr.body[1:]:
        if stop(node): break
        out.append(ast.dump(node,include_attributes=False))
    return out
pold=prefix(old,lambda n:isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t,ast.Name) and t.id=='npass' for t in n.targets))
pnew=prefix(new,lambda n:isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='POST')
print('Existing probe AST unchanged before added POST sessions:',pold==pnew)
logroot=root/'regress/artifacts/crimewidthkill/killtable11_20260916/logs'
checked=[]
for log in sorted(logroot.glob('probe_*.raw')):
    t=log.read_text()
    score=re.search(r'CRIMEP_RESULT=(\\d+)/(\\d+)',t)
    fail=re.search(r'"'^FAILING: *(.*)$'"',t,re.M)[1].strip()
    red=[] if not fail else fail.split(', ')
    assert score and int(score[1])+len(red)==int(score[2])==188
    assert t.count('CRIMEP_DONE')==1
    checked.append((log.stem.removeprefix('probe_'),int(score[1]),red))
print('Archived raw logs checked:',len(checked))
print('Generated mutants checked:',sum(n.startswith('C_') for n,_,_ in checked))
print('Generated survivors:',[(n,s) for n,s,_ in checked if n.startswith('C_') and s==188])
for n,s,red in checked:
    if n.startswith('C_'): print(n,188-s,','.join(red))

codex
I found an omitted CONTROL branch: the probe never issues an accepted HARD_RESET write after the latch is set. A guard that rejects only that case appears able to lose the reset effect while preserving every current row. I’m marking that as a static prediction; the archived results do confirm all 28 generated cases.

root=pathlib.Path('.')
base=(root/'src/devices/dev_sgi_ip32.c').read_text()

expected={'rev':'CRIME_REV','ctl':'CRIME_CONTROL','ist':'CRIME_INTSTAT','imk':'CRIME_INTMASK','dog':'CRIME_DOG','tim':'CRIME_TIME'}
checked=0
for lab in list(expected)+['dfl']:
    expr='relative_addr == '+expected[lab] if lab"'!='"'dfl' else 'relative_addr "'!= CRIME_REV && relative_addr != CRIME_CONTROL &&'"\\n\\t    relative_addr "'!= CRIME_INTSTAT && relative_addr != CRIME_INTMASK &&'"\\n\\t    relative_addr "'!= CRIME_DOG && relative_addr != CRIME_TIME'"'
    for size in (4,8):
        anchor='\\tif (len == 8) {' if size==8 else '\\t} else if (len == 4) {'
        head='\\tif (len == 8' if size==8 else '\\t} else if (len == 4'
        assert base.count(anchor+'\\n')==1
        for direction,code in [('r','MEM_READ'),('w','MEM_WRITE')]:
            name='C_%s%d%s'%(lab,size,direction)
            diff=(root/'regress/artifacts/crimewidthkill/killtable11_20260916/logs'/(name+'.diff')).read_text()
            removed=[l[1:] for l in diff.splitlines() if l.startswith('-') and not l.startswith('---')]
            added='\\n'.join(l[1:] for l in diff.splitlines() if l.startswith('+') and not l.startswith('+++'))
            want=head+' && "'!(writeflag == '"'+code+' && ('+expr+') &&\\n\\t    (d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN))) {'
            assert removed==[anchor],(name,removed)
            assert added==want,(name,added,want)
            checked+=1
print('Archived diffs exactly match all width/direction/label K-form edits:',checked)
probe=ast.parse((root/'regress/crime_width_probe.py').read_text())
accepts=next(ast.literal_eval(n.value) for n in probe.body if isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t,ast.Name) and t.id=='ACCEPTS' for t in n.targets))
def bucket(e):
    op,off,v=e
    if off==0 and op in ('sd','sw','lw'): return 'equivalent'
    if off==8 and (op=='lw' or op in ('sw','sd') and v in (0,0x100,0x200)): return 'equivalent'
    if off==8 and op in ('sd','sw') and v in (1,0x201) or off==4 and op=='sw': return 'host'
    if (op,off) in [('lw',0x10),('ld',0x18),('lw',0x18),('ld',0x30),('ld',0x38),('sd',0x40),('ld',0x40),('ld',0x278)]: return 'existing'
    return 'new-guest'
from collections import Counter
print('Independent ACCEPTS semantic classification:',dict(Counter(map(bucket,accepts))))
for b in ('equivalent','host','existing','new-guest'):
    print(b+': '+'; '.join('%s +0x%x value=0x%x'%e for e in accepts if bucket(e)==b))

The audit is complete. The 28 archived diffs match the generator, all 33 result logs reconcile, and `latch said` has the required command order. The blocking issue is the omitted HARD_RESET branch. The coverage record also needs correction: three of the five host-diagnostic entries now have post-latch checks.
codex
ENUMERATION WRONG: latch-set CONTROL writes with HARD_RESET set. CRIME_CLOSE8_20260916

1. **The generator exhausts its stated product, but that product omits a source-tested branch.** I verified all 28 generated diffs and reconciled all 33 archived result logs: 27 generated mutants fail; `C_ctl4r` passes consistently with its guest equivalence.

   The six case labels plus default are complete. They do not exhaust the function’s distinctions: the handler also tests the 32-bit lane, REV’s PC window, and CONTROL’s data bits. The consequential omission here is [CONTROL’s HARD_RESET test](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_ip32.c:364). Killing a mutant that rejects *every* post-latch CONTROL write does not establish coverage of rejection confined to HARD_RESET.

2. **Here is the missing guard-growth candidate.** Replace the opening of the 4-byte arm at [line 280](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_ip32.c:280) with:

   ```c
   } else if (len == 4 &&
       !(writeflag == MEM_WRITE &&
         relative_addr == CRIME_CONTROL &&
         (idata & CRIME_CONTROL_HARD_RESET) &&
         (d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN))) {
   ```

   **Predicted result: 188/188; not measured.** Reconstructing all 51 sessions found exactly one accepted CONTROL write carrying HARD_RESET: `sw 0x0800` in `sw08 halts`, with the latch **clear**. Every post-latch accepted CONTROL write carries one of `0`, `1`, `0x100`, `0x200`, or `0x201`. Consequently, this added rejection condition never triggers in the probe.

   The guest-visible difference is direct: after a refused access, an accepted `sw 0x0800` should stop the CPUs; this mutant silently drops it. **`sw08 halts` is the expected catching row, but its latch state excludes the mutant.**

   The disclosure about a re-arm/pre-set *inside* HARD_RESET being unrowable does not discharge this case. Preventing the halt is observable using the existing halt-control technique. HARD_RESET is also a single tested bit, without the disclosed DOG-plus-leftover joint.

3. **The 46-entry semantic classification checks out; its coverage description does not.** I found no incorrectly classified guest-equivalent entry among the ten, and the eight existing post-latch pins have actual value/effect checks.

   However, “5 host-diagnostic entries … disclosed rather than rowed” is false. [The added CONTROL message count](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:1211) checks three of them:

   | Host-diagnostic entry | Current post-latch coverage |
   |---|---|
   | CONTROL `sd 0x0001` | `p acc ctl msg` |
   | CONTROL `sw 0x0001` | `p acc ctl msg` |
   | CONTROL `sw 0x0201` | `p acc ctl msg` |
   | CONTROL `sd 0x0201` | Unrowed |
   | Default `sw +0x04` | Unrowed; executing the write does not check its diagnostic |

   Thus the coverage accounting is **23 newly checked guest entries + 8 previously checked guest entries + 10 equivalent + 3 checked host-diagnostic + 2 unrowed host-diagnostic = 46**.

   The announced-form argument holds: reconstruction places all 46 ACCEPTS operations before the first `lh +0x06`; their width-4/8 notices cannot match that width-2 notice.

4. **The three `CRIMEP_CONTROL=FAIL` legs are attributable kills.** The archived values agree with the edits:

   - `C_ist4r` and `C_imk4r`: the setup checks pass; the subsequent accepted read fails to reassert IP2.
   - `C_tim8r`: accepted TIME reads return zero, failing the four `time * ctl` rows and three additional checks.

   I found no invalid-session evidence explaining these failures. The matching baseline archive reports **188/188, CONTROL=OK**.

5. **Two further substantive statements are wrong.**

   - [“Every value row this probe had was measured with the latch CLEAR”](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:203) remains false. `imask keeps`, `sb keeps`, `sh keeps`, and the TIME controls already operated after refusals. The cited previous report explicitly corrected this assertion.
   - [“The latch-CLEAR half is table 10’s twelve S_* silent bypasses”](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:211) overstates that evidence. Those twelve predicates are unconditional on the latch and cover selected cells; they do not establish the entire 28-cell clear-state half for latch-conditioned silent bypasses.

The current probe matches the archived Table 11 instrument, and its pre-existing executable AST is unchanged before the added sessions. I ran no gate or emulator. The new candidate needs measurement because this workspace is read-only: run **`K_ctl4w_reset_post`** against the 188-row probe, then verify added post-latch HARD_RESET controls at both accepted widths. The closure denominator must include that branch.

NO-GO
